Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The biological variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 588 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 553 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 588 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the biological structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 553. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
